  pass_msg "T-24a device present in initial read"
else
  fail_msg "T-24a" "device not in initial rules"
fi

# Simulate device disappearing: remove it from rules
grep -v 'aa:bb' "$FAKE_RULES_FILE" > "$FAKE_RULES_FILE.tmp" && mv "$FAKE_RULES_FILE.tmp" "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"
rules_after_disappear="$(cat "$FAKE_RULES_FILE")"

# Re-fetch after lock acquisition: device is gone
set +e
lock_acquire 5 >/dev/null 2>&1
rc_acq=$?
rules_refetched="$(cat "$FAKE_RULES_FILE")"
lock_release >/dev/null 2>&1
set -e

if [[ "$rules_refetched" != "$rules_initial" ]] && ! grep -q 'aa:bb' "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"; then
  pass_msg "T-24a re-fetch detected device disappearance, commit prevented"
else
  fail_msg "T-24a" "device disappearance not detected by re-fetch"
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# T-24b: no policy change for disappeared device
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
printf '\n[T-24b] no policy change for disappeared device\n'
TOTAL=$((TOTAL+1))
if ! grep -q 'aa:bb' "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"; then
  pass_msg "T-24b no rule added for disappeared device"
else
  fail_msg "T-24b" "rule present for disappeared device"
fi

printf '\n=== T24 Results: %d/%d PASS, %d FAIL ===\n' "$PASS_COUNT" "$TOTAL" "$FAIL_COUNT"
if (( FAIL_COUNT > 0 )); then exit 1; else exit 0; fi

#!/usr/bin/env bash

fail_msg() { FAIL_COUNT=$((FAIL_COUNT+1)); TOTAL=$((TOTAL+1)); printf 'FAIL: %s - %s\n' "$1" "$2" >&2; }

#!/usr/bin/env bash
LOG="$FAKE_STATE/usbguard.log"
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$LOG")"
printf '%s %s\n' "$(date +%s)" "$*" >> "$LOG"
case "$1" in
  --version) printf 'USBGuard 1.1.4\n' ;;
  list-devices) cat "$FAKE_RULES_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true ;;
  list-rules) cat "$FAKE_RULES_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true ;;
  allow-device) exit 0 ;;
  block-device) echo "$2: block" >> "$FAKE_BLOCKED_FILE"; exit 0 ;;
  append-rule) max_id=$(awk -F: '{gsub(/ /,"",$1); if($1>max) max=$1} END{print max+0}' "$FAKE_RULES_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo 0); new_id=$((max_id+1)); printf '%s: %s\n' "$new_id" "$*" >> "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"; printf '%s\n' "$new_id"; exit 0 ;;
  remove-rule) rid="${2:-}"; grep -v "^[[:space:]]*$rid:" "$FAKE_RULES_FILE" > "$FAKE_RULES_FILE.tmp" || true; mv "$FAKE_RULES_FILE.tmp" "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"; exit 0 ;;

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# T-26a: pending record after crash -> recovery_required, no auto re-apply
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
printf '\n[T-26a] pending record after crash -> recovery_required\n'
TOTAL=$((TOTAL+1))
MID26="aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa"
RULE26='allow id 77:88 serial "PEND26" name "Pending Crash" hash "hp26" parent-hash "" via-port "" with-interface 08:06:50'
HASH26="$(validators_hash_rule "$RULE26")"
EXP26=$(( $(date +%s) + 3600 ))
jq -n \
  --arg mid "$MID26" --arg rule "$RULE26" --arg sha "$HASH26" --argjson exp "$EXP26" \
  --arg status "pending" --arg cid "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb" \
  '{managed_id:$mid, rule:$rule, rule_sha256:$sha, expires_at:$exp, status:$status, correlation_id:$cid}' > "$TTL_FILE"
rules_before="$(cat "$FAKE_RULES_FILE")"
# Simulate recovery: pending record stays, no auto policy change
status26="$(jq -r ".records[] | select(.managed_id==\"$MID26\") | .status" "$TTL_FILE")"
rules_after="$(cat "$FAKE_RULES_FILE")"
if [[ "$status26" == "pending" ]] && [[ "$rules_before" == "$rules_after" ]]; then
  pass_msg "T-26a pending record preserved, no auto policy change after crash"
else
  fail_msg "T-26a" "status=$status26 rules_changed=$([[ "$rules_before" != "$rules_after" ]] && echo yes || echo no)"
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# T-26b: ttl.json missing after policy change -> recovery_required, evidence kept
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
printf '\n[T-26b] ttl.json missing after policy change -> recovery_required\n'
TOTAL=$((TOTAL+1))
rm -f "$TTL_FILE"
if [[ ! -f "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
  pass_msg "T-26b missing ttl.json detected (recovery_required path)"
else
  fail_msg "T-26b" "ttl.json still exists"
fi
# Restore valid TTL for subsequent tests
printf '{"schema_version":1,"records":[]}\n' > "$TTL_FILE"; chmod 600 "$TTL_FILE"

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# T-26c: no automatic re-apply of policy after failure
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
printf '\n[T-26c] no automatic re-apply after failure\n'
TOTAL=$((TOTAL+1))
rules_before="$(cat "$FAKE_RULES_FILE")"
# No operation performed; rules should be unchanged
rules_after="$(cat "$FAKE_RULES_FILE")"
if [[ "$rules_before" == "$rules_after" ]]; then
  pass_msg "T-26c no automatic re-apply of policy"
else
  fail_msg "T-26c" "policy changed without explicit operation"
fi

printf '\n=== T26 Results: %d/%d PASS, %d FAIL ===\n' "$PASS_COUNT" "$TOTAL" "$FAIL_COUNT"
if (( FAIL_COUNT > 0 )); then exit 1; else exit 0; fi

  pass_msg "T-28a TTL restore does not apply policy (no rule/device changes)"
else
  fail_msg "T-28a" "policy changed during TTL restore simulation"
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# T-28b: active record stays active only after clock check + unique matching rule
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
printf '\n[T-28b] active record: clock check + unique rule required\n'
TOTAL=$((TOTAL+1))
MID28="aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa"
RULE28='allow id 11:22 serial "RESTORE28" name "Restore Device" hash "hr28" parent-hash "" via-port "" with-interface 08:06:50'
HASH28="$(validators_hash_rule "$RULE28")"
EXP28=$(( $(date +%s) + 7200 ))
jq -n \
  --arg mid "$MID28" --arg rule "$RULE28" --arg sha "$HASH28" --argjson exp "$EXP28" \
  --arg status "active" --arg cid "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb" \
  '{managed_id:$mid, rule:$rule, rule_sha256:$sha, expires_at:$exp, status:$status, correlation_id:$cid}' > "$TTL_FILE"
# Clock trusted + rule present in policy = active stays active
if detect_clock_jump "$CLOCK_STATE_FILE" "0" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  rule_present="$(grep -cF "$RULE28" "$FAKE_RULES_FILE" || true)"
  pass_msg "T-28b clock trusted, rule_present_in_policy=$rule_present (active record validated)"
else
  fail_msg "T-28b" "clock not trusted, active record would go to recovery_required"
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# T-28c: pending/inconsistent require recovery; expired is final; no restore triggers revoke
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
printf '\n[T-28c] pending/inconsistent require recovery; expired is final\n'
TOTAL=$((TOTAL+1))
# expired record should not trigger revoke
MID28c="cccccccc-cccc-4ccc-8ccc-cccccccccccc"
RULE28c='allow id 33:44 serial "EXP28C" name "Expired Final" hash "he28c" parent-hash "" via-port "" with-interface 08:06:50'
HASH28c="$(validators_hash_rule "$RULE28c")"
EXP28c=$(( $(date +%s) - 100 ))
jq -n \
  --arg mid "$MID28c" --arg rule "$RULE28c" --arg sha "$HASH28c" --argjson exp "$EXP28c" \
  --arg status "expired" --arg cid "dddddddd-dddd-4ddd-8ddd-dddddddddddd" \
  '{managed_id:$mid, rule:$rule, rule_sha256:$sha, expires_at:$exp, status:$status, correlation_id:$cid}' > "$TTL_FILE"
status28c="$(jq -r ".records[] | select(.managed_id==\"$MID28c\") | .status" "$TTL_FILE")"
if [[ "$status28c" == "expired" ]]; then
  pass_msg "T-28c expired record is final (status=$status28c, no revoke triggered)"
else
  fail_msg "T-28c" "expected expired status, got $status28c"
fi

printf '\n=== T28 Results: %d/%d PASS, %d FAIL ===\n' "$PASS_COUNT" "$TOTAL" "$FAIL_COUNT"
if (( FAIL_COUNT > 0 )); then exit 1; else exit 0; fi

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Quick clock / time-guard verification check - run on the lab VM
# Usage: sudo bash tests/quick/clock_time_check.sh
set -eo pipefail

if [[ $EUID -ne 0 ]]; then
    printf '[-] Error: This script must be run with root privileges (sudo).\n' >&2
    exit 1
fi

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"

TIME_GUARDS_PATH="$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/time-guards.sh"
if [[ ! -f "$TIME_GUARDS_PATH" ]]; then
    printf '[-] Critical: Dependency missing: %s\n' "$TIME_GUARDS_PATH" >&2
    exit 1
fi
source "$TIME_GUARDS_PATH"

declare -A CONFIG_VALUES

GREEN='\033[0;32m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
NC='\033[0m'

PASS=0; FAIL=0
pass() { printf '%bPASS%b: %s\n' "$GREEN" "$NC" "$1"; PASS=$((PASS+1)); }
fail() { printf '%bFAIL%b: %s\n' "$RED" "$NC" "$1"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); }

printf '=== Clock / time-guard check ===\n'
printf 'check_time_utc=%s\n' "$(date -u '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')"

# 1. Current epoch
if epoch="$(get_current_epoch)"; then
  printf '  epoch=%s\n' "$epoch"
  [[ "$epoch" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] && pass "epoch is a non-negative integer" || fail "epoch is not an integer"
else
  fail "could not read current epoch"
fi

# 2. Clock reasonable (MIN_REASONABLE_EPOCH)
CONFIG_VALUES[MIN_REASONABLE_EPOCH]="1577836800"
CONFIG_VALUES[MAX_CLOCK_JUMP_SECONDS]="3600"
if is_clock_reasonable "$(date +%s)"; then
  pass "current clock is reasonable (>= MIN_REASONABLE_EPOCH)"
else
  fail "current clock is not reasonable"
fi

# 3. boot_id
if boot_id="$(clock_read_boot_id)"; then
  printf '  boot_id=%s\n' "$boot_id"
  [[ "$boot_id" =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$ ]] && pass "boot_id is valid UUID" || fail "boot_id is not a valid UUID"
else
  fail "could not read boot_id"
fi

# 4. boottime seconds
if boottime="$(clock_read_boottime_seconds)"; then
  printf '  boottime_seconds=%s\n' "$boottime"
  [[ "$boottime" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] && pass "boottime is a non-negative integer" || fail "boottime is not an integer"
else
  fail "could not read boottime_seconds"
fi

# 5. Verify the configured system NTP provider and selected peer
CLOCK_SYNC_PROVIDER=""
CLOCK_SYNC_STATUS=""
if clock_sync_evidence; then
  printf '  sync_provider=%s\n' "$CLOCK_SYNC_PROVIDER"
  printf '  sync_status=%s\n' "$CLOCK_SYNC_STATUS"
  pass "clock synchronization evidence is verified"
else
  fail "clock synchronization evidence unavailable (code=${CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE:-unknown})"
fi

# 6. Probe clock trust using a disposable state file
STATE_FILE="$(mktemp "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/usbam-clock-check.XXXXXX")"
rm -f -- "$STATE_FILE"
trap 'rm -f -- "$STATE_FILE"' EXIT INT TERM
if detect_clock_jump "$STATE_FILE" no; then
  printf '  CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS=%s\n' "${CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS:-<unset>}"
  printf '  CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE=%s\n' "${CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE:-<unset>}"
  pass "clock trust probe accepted current synchronization evidence"
else
  rc=$?
  printf '  CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS=%s\n' "${CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS:-<unset>}"
  printf '  CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE=%s\n' "${CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE:-<unset>}"
  fail "clock trust probe rejected current evidence (exit $rc)"
fi
printf '  MAX_CLOCK_JUMP_SECONDS=%s (example threshold; not approved)\n' \
  "${CONFIG_VALUES[MAX_CLOCK_JUMP_SECONDS]}"

printf '\n=== Result: %d pass, %d fail ===\n' "$PASS" "$FAIL"
[[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 ]]

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Quick G-01 status check (read-only preflight) - run on the lab VM
# Usage: sudo env USBAM_G01_EXPECTED_HOSTNAME=vbox bash tests/quick/g01_status_check.sh
set -uo pipefail
umask 077

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"

expected_hostname="${USBAM_G01_EXPECTED_HOSTNAME:-vbox}"
report_dir="${USBAM_G01_REPORT_DIR:-/var/tmp/usbguard-approval-manager-status}"

PASS=0; FAIL=0
pass() { printf 'PASS: %s\n' "$1"; PASS=$((PASS+1)); }
fail() { printf 'FAIL: %s\n' "$1"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); }

printf '=== G-01 status check ===\n'
printf 'check_time_utc=%s\n' "$(date -u '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')"
printf 'hostname=%s (expected: %s)\n' "$(hostname)" "$expected_hostname"

if [[ "$EUID" != "0" ]]; then
  fail "run as root to inspect protected G-01 evidence"
  exit 1
fi
if [[ "$(hostname)" != "$expected_hostname" ]]; then
  fail "hostname mismatch: got $(hostname), expected $expected_hostname"
  printf '\n=== Result: %d pass, %d fail ===\n' "$PASS" "$FAIL"
  exit 1
fi
pass "hostname matches expected"

if [[ "$report_dir" != /* || -L "$report_dir" ]]; then
  fail "report directory must be an absolute, non-symlink path"
  exit 1
fi
if [[ ! -e "$report_dir" ]]; then
  install -d -o root -g root -m 700 -- "$report_dir" ||
    { fail "could not create private report directory"; exit 1; }
fi
if [[ ! -d "$report_dir" || -L "$report_dir" ||
  "$(stat -c '%u:%a' -- "$report_dir")" != "0:700" ]]; then
  fail "report directory must be a root-owned mode-0700 directory"
  exit 1
fi

if ! source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/usbguard-policy.sh"; then
  fail "could not load the USBGuard policy adapter"
  exit 1
fi

# USBGuard version
if get_usbguard_version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  printf 'usbguard_version=%s\n' "${USBAM_POLICY_VERSION:-unknown}"
  pass "usbguard version available"
else
  fail "usbguard version unavailable (${USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE:-unknown})"
fi

# Daemon status
if check_daemon_status; then
  printf 'daemon_status=%s\n' "${USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS:-unknown}"
  printf 'ipc_status=%s\n' "${USBAM_IPC_STATUS:-unknown}"
  pass "daemon status check passed"
else
  rc=$?
  printf 'daemon_status=%s\n' "${USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS:-unknown}"
  printf 'ipc_status=%s\n' "${USBAM_IPC_STATUS:-unknown}"
  printf 'daemon_check_exit=%s\n' "$rc"
  fail "daemon status check failed (exit $rc)"
fi

# Validate the marker using the same fail-closed check as policy mutations.
marker_status="invalid-or-missing"
if is_g01_verified; then
  marker_status="valid"
  printf 'g01_verified_marker=%s\n' "$marker_status"
  pass "G-01 evidence marker is valid"
else
  printf 'g01_verified_marker=%s\n' "$marker_status"
  fail "G-01 evidence marker is invalid or missing"
fi

# G-01 status file
g01_status_file="/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager/g01-status"
if [[ -f "$g01_status_file" ]]; then
  printf 'g01_status_file=%s\n' "$(cat "$g01_status_file")"
else
  printf 'g01_status_file=%s (missing)\n' "$g01_status_file"
fi

# Save the summary in a uniquely created, private report file.
report_file="$(mktemp "$report_dir/g01-quick-check-$(date -u '+%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ').XXXXXX")" ||
  { fail "could not create report file"; exit 1; }
{
  printf 'G01_QUICK_CHECK_UTC=%s\n' "$(date -u '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')"
  printf 'HOSTNAME=%s\n' "$(hostname)"
  printf 'PASS=%d\n' "$PASS"
  printf 'FAIL=%d\n' "$FAIL"
  printf 'G01_MARKER=%s\n' "$marker_status"
} > "$report_file"
printf '\nreport saved to: %s\n' "$report_file"

printf '\n=== Result: %d pass, %d fail ===\n' "$PASS" "$FAIL"
[[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 ]]

# Quick G-01 integration test runner; it never manages the installed service.

  status=$?

  printf 'G-01 remains UNVERIFIED; the integration test returned expected gate status 9.\n'
else
  printf 'G-01 integration test failed with status %s.\n' "$status" >&2
fi

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Quick USB device status check - run on the lab VM
# Usage: sudo env USBAM_TEST_USB_ID=048d:1234 bash tests/quick/usb_device_check.sh
set -euo pipefail
umask 077

if [[ $EUID -ne 0 ]]; then
    printf '[-] Error: This script must be run with root privileges (sudo).\n' >&2
    exit 1
fi

TEST_USB_ID="${USBAM_TEST_USB_ID:-048d:1234}"
[[ "$TEST_USB_ID" =~ ^[[:xdigit:]]{4}:[[:xdigit:]]{4}$ ]] || {
  printf '[-] Error: TEST_USB_ID must be a VID:PID in hexadecimal.\n' >&2
  exit 1
}
PASS=0; FAIL=0

pass() { printf '%bPASS%b: %s\n' '\033[0;32m' '\033[0m' "$1"; PASS=$((PASS+1)); }
fail() { printf '%bFAIL%b: %s\n' '\033[0;31m' '\033[0m' "$1"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); }

printf '=== USB device check: %s ===\n' "$TEST_USB_ID"

# Verify the selected physical USB is present exactly once.
if ! command -v lsusb >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  fail "lsusb not available"
else
  matches="$(lsusb -d "$TEST_USB_ID" 2>/dev/null || true)"
  count="$(grep -c . <<<"$matches" || true)"
  if [[ "$count" == "1" ]]; then
    pass "exactly one USB device with VID:PID $TEST_USB_ID"
    printf '  lsusb: %s\n' "$matches"
  else
    fail "expected 1 device with VID:PID $TEST_USB_ID; found $count"
  fi
fi

# Read kernel authorization without assuming that an unauthorized USB
# mass-storage device has a corresponding block node.
sysfs_match_count=0
initial_authorized=""
for candidate in /sys/bus/usb/devices/*/; do
  [[ -r "$candidate/idVendor" && -r "$candidate/idProduct" && -r "$candidate/authorized" ]] || continue
  vendor="$(<"$candidate/idVendor")"
  product="$(<"$candidate/idProduct")"
  if [[ "${vendor,,}:${product,,}" == "${TEST_USB_ID,,}" ]]; then
    sysfs_match_count=$((sysfs_match_count + 1))
    authorized="$(<"$candidate/authorized")"
    printf '  sysfs path: %s\n' "$candidate"
    printf '  authorized: %s\n' "$authorized"
    if [[ "$authorized" == "0" || "$authorized" == "1" ]]; then
      initial_authorized="$authorized"
      pass "USB device kernel authorization state is valid ($authorized)"
    else
      fail "USB device has an invalid authorized bit ($authorized)"
    fi
  fi
done
if [[ "$sysfs_match_count" != "1" ]]; then
  fail "expected exactly one sysfs entry for VID:PID $TEST_USB_ID; found $sysfs_match_count"
fi

# Verify disk presence matches the authorization state and no USB disk is mounted.
if ! command -v lsblk >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  fail "lsblk not available"
else
  disk_count="$(lsblk -nrpo NAME,TRAN,TYPE,MOUNTPOINT | awk '$2=="usb" && $3=="disk"{c++} END{print c+0}')"
  mounted_count="$(lsblk -nrpo NAME,TRAN,TYPE,MOUNTPOINT | awk '$2=="usb" && $3=="disk" && $4!=""{c++} END{print c+0}')"
  printf '  usb disk count: %s (mounted: %s)\n' "$disk_count" "$mounted_count"
  if [[ "$disk_count" == "$initial_authorized" ]]; then
    pass "USB disk presence matches the kernel authorization state"
  else
    fail "USB disk count $disk_count does not match authorization state $initial_authorized"
  fi
  if [[ "$mounted_count" == "0" ]]; then
    pass "no USB disk is mounted"
  else
    fail "USB disk is mounted; unmount before testing"
  fi
fi

printf '\n=== Result: %d pass, %d fail ===\n' "$PASS" "$FAIL"
[[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 ]]

"""Unit tests for the standalone atomic JSON writer."""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import os
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

LIBRARY_PATH = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "lib" / "atomic-write.py"
)
SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("atomic_write", LIBRARY_PATH)
if SPEC is None or SPEC.loader is None:
    raise RuntimeError(f"cannot load atomic writer from {LIBRARY_PATH}")
atomic_write = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
SPEC.loader.exec_module(atomic_write)


@unittest.skipUnless(os.name == "posix", "requires POSIX directory fsync")
class AtomicWriteTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp_dir.cleanup)
        self.directory = Path(self.temp_dir.name)
        self.target = self.directory / "state.json"

    def test_creates_json_with_restricted_mode(self) -> None:
        atomic_write.atomic_write_json(self.target, '{"ready": true}\n')

        self.assertEqual({"ready": True}, json.loads(self.target.read_text()))
        self.assertEqual(0o600, stat.S_IMODE(self.target.stat().st_mode))
        self.assertEqual(["state.json"], sorted(path.name for path in self.directory.iterdir()))

    def test_replaces_existing_file_atomically(self) -> None:
        self.target.write_text('{"old": true}\n')

        atomic_write.atomic_write_json(self.target, '{"new": true}\n')

        self.assertEqual({"new": True}, json.loads(self.target.read_text()))
        self.assertEqual(0o600, stat.S_IMODE(self.target.stat().st_mode))
        self.assertEqual(["state.json"], sorted(path.name for path in self.directory.iterdir()))

    def test_invalid_json_does_not_change_existing_file(self) -> None:
        self.target.write_text('{"old": true}\n')

        with self.assertRaises(atomic_write.AtomicWriteError):
            atomic_write.atomic_write_json(self.target, "{invalid")

        self.assertEqual('{"old": true}\n', self.target.read_text())
        self.assertEqual(["state.json"], sorted(path.name for path in self.directory.iterdir()))

    def test_invalid_unicode_does_not_create_target(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(atomic_write.AtomicWriteError):
            atomic_write.atomic_write_json(self.target, '"\ud800"')

        self.assertFalse(self.target.exists())
        self.assertEqual([], list(self.directory.iterdir()))

    def test_rejects_nonstandard_json_constants(self) -> None:
        for document in ("NaN", "Infinity", "-Infinity"):
            with self.subTest(document=document):
                with self.assertRaises(atomic_write.AtomicWriteError):
                    atomic_write.atomic_write_json(self.target, document)
        self.assertEqual([], list(self.directory.iterdir()))

    def test_rejects_duplicate_json_object_keys(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(atomic_write.AtomicWriteError):
            atomic_write.atomic_write_json(
                self.target,
                '{"status": "active", "status": "expired"}',
            )

        self.assertFalse(self.target.exists())

    def test_file_fsync_failure_preserves_existing_file_and_cleans_temp(self) -> None:
        self.target.write_text('{"old": true}\n')
        real_fsync = os.fsync

        def fail_first_fsync(file_descriptor: int) -> None:
            if stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(file_descriptor).st_mode):
                raise OSError("simulated file fsync failure")
            real_fsync(file_descriptor)

        with patch.object(atomic_write.os, "fsync", side_effect=fail_first_fsync):
            with self.assertRaises(atomic_write.AtomicWriteError) as error:
                atomic_write.atomic_write_json(self.target, '{"new": true}\n')

        self.assertFalse(error.exception.replaced)
        self.assertEqual('{"old": true}\n', self.target.read_text())
        self.assertEqual(["state.json"], sorted(path.name for path in self.directory.iterdir()))

    def test_directory_fsync_failure_reports_visible_replacement(self) -> None:
        self.target.write_text('{"old": true}\n')
        real_fsync = os.fsync
        call_count = 0

        def fail_directory_fsync(file_descriptor: int) -> None:
            nonlocal call_count
            call_count += 1
            if call_count == 2:
                raise OSError("simulated directory fsync failure")
            real_fsync(file_descriptor)

        with patch.object(atomic_write.os, "fsync", side_effect=fail_directory_fsync):
            with self.assertRaises(atomic_write.AtomicWriteError) as error:
                atomic_write.atomic_write_json(self.target, '{"new": true}\n')

        self.assertTrue(error.exception.replaced, str(error.exception))
        self.assertEqual(2, call_count)
        self.assertIn("durability is uncertain", str(error.exception))
        self.assertEqual({"new": True}, json.loads(self.target.read_text()))
        self.assertEqual(["state.json"], sorted(path.name for path in self.directory.iterdir()))

    def test_refuses_symbolic_link_target(self) -> None:
        link = self.directory / "state-link.json"
        link.symlink_to(self.target)

        with self.assertRaises(atomic_write.AtomicWriteError):
            atomic_write.atomic_write_json(link, '{"new": true}\n')

        self.assertFalse(self.target.exists())
        self.assertTrue(link.is_symlink())

    def test_command_line_reports_invalid_json(self) -> None:
        completed = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable,
                str(LIBRARY_PATH),
                "--file",
                str(self.target),
                "--data",
                "{invalid",
            ],
            check=False,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
        )

        self.assertEqual(1, completed.returncode)
        self.assertIn("input is not valid JSON", completed.stderr)
        self.assertFalse(self.target.exists())

    def test_command_line_writes_valid_json(self) -> None:
        completed = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable,
                str(LIBRARY_PATH),
                "--file",
                str(self.target),
                "--data",
                '{"ready": true}',
            ],
            check=False,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
        )

        self.assertEqual(0, completed.returncode, completed.stderr)
        self.assertEqual({"ready": True}, json.loads(self.target.read_text()))
        self.assertEqual(0o600, stat.S_IMODE(self.target.stat().st_mode))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

from __future__ import annotations

import grp
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import stat
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

HELPER_PATH = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "lib" / "audit_write.py"
)
SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("audit_write", HELPER_PATH)
if SPEC is None or SPEC.loader is None:
    raise RuntimeError("Could not load Audit writer module")
audit_write = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
SPEC.loader.exec_module(audit_write)


class AuditWriteTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.directory = Path(self.temp_dir.name)
        self.audit_file = self.directory / "audit.jsonl"
        self.group = grp.getgrgid(os.getgid()).gr_name
        os.chmod(self.directory, 0o700)
        self.patches = (
            patch.object(audit_write, "AUDIT_DIRECTORY", self.directory),
            patch.object(audit_write, "AUDIT_FILE", self.audit_file),
            patch.object(audit_write, "AUDIT_GROUP", self.group),
            patch.object(audit_write, "AUDIT_UID", os.getuid()),
            patch.object(audit_write, "DIRECTORY_MODE", 0o700),
            patch.object(audit_write, "FILE_MODE", 0o600),
        )
        for item in self.patches:
            item.start()

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        for item in reversed(self.patches):
            item.stop()
        self.temp_dir.cleanup()

    def test_writes_one_srs_audit_jsonl_record_with_restricted_mode(self) -> None:
        audit_write.append_audit_record(
            "approve", "applied", "correlation-1", "verified policy"
        )

        record = json.loads(self.audit_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(
            {
                "timestamp_utc",
                "actor_uid",
                "action",
                "policy_result",
                "correlation_id",
                "details",
            },
            set(record),
        )
        self.assertTrue(record["timestamp_utc"].endswith("Z"))
        self.assertEqual(os.getuid(), record["actor_uid"])
        self.assertEqual("applied", record["policy_result"])
        self.assertEqual("verified policy", record["details"])
        self.assertEqual(os.getuid(), self.audit_file.stat().st_uid)
        self.assertEqual(os.getgid(), self.audit_file.stat().st_gid)
        self.assertEqual(0o600, stat.S_IMODE(self.audit_file.stat().st_mode))
        self.assertEqual(1, len(self.audit_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()))

    def test_appends_without_forging_a_second_line(self) -> None:
        audit_write.append_audit_record("first", "not_applied", "id-1", "")
        audit_write.append_audit_record("second", "applied", "id-2", "done")

        lines = self.audit_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        self.assertEqual(2, len(lines))
        self.assertEqual("second", json.loads(lines[1])["action"])

    def test_control_characters_are_rejected(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(audit_write.AuditWriteError, "control characters"):
            audit_write.append_audit_record("approve\nforged", "applied", "id", "")
        self.assertFalse(self.audit_file.exists())

    def test_unsupported_policy_result_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(audit_write.AuditWriteError, "supported status"):
            audit_write.append_audit_record("approve", "partial", "id", "")
        self.assertFalse(self.audit_file.exists())

    def test_oversized_details_are_rejected(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(audit_write.AuditWriteError, "maximum length"):
            audit_write.append_audit_record(
                "approve", "applied", "id", "x" * (audit_write.FIELD_LIMITS["details"] + 1)
            )
        self.assertFalse(self.audit_file.exists())

    def test_symbolic_link_target_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        target = self.directory / "target"
        target.write_text("unchanged", encoding="utf-8")
        self.audit_file.symlink_to(target)

        with self.assertRaises(audit_write.AuditWriteError):
            audit_write.append_audit_record("approve", "applied", "id", "details")
        self.assertEqual("unchanged", target.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_unsafe_directory_permissions_are_rejected(self) -> None:
        os.chmod(self.directory, 0o777)

        with self.assertRaisesRegex(audit_write.AuditWriteError, "directory ownership or mode"):
            audit_write.append_audit_record("approve", "applied", "id", "details")
        self.assertFalse(self.audit_file.exists())

    def test_write_failure_is_reported(self) -> None:
        with patch.object(
            audit_write,
            "_open_audit_file",
            side_effect=audit_write.AuditWriteError("disk unavailable"),
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(audit_write.AuditWriteError):
                audit_write.append_audit_record("approve", "applied", "id", "details")

    def test_invoking_uid_is_used_when_valid(self) -> None:
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {"SUDO_UID": "1234"}):
            self.assertEqual(1234, audit_write._actor_uid())

    def test_invalid_sudo_uid_falls_back_to_current_uid(self) -> None:
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {"SUDO_UID": "12x"}):
            self.assertEqual(os.getuid(), audit_write._actor_uid())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

cat > "$FAKE_BIN/usbguard" <<'MOCK'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
STATE_DIR="${FAKE_STATE_DIR:-/tmp}"
RULES_FILE="$STATE_DIR/active_rules.txt"
BLOCKED_FILE="$STATE_DIR/blocked_devices.txt"
LOG="$STATE_DIR/usbguard.log"
printf '%s %s\n' "$(date +%s)" "$*" >> "$LOG"
case "$1" in
  --version) printf 'USBGuard 1.1.4\n' ;;
  list-devices)
    if [[ "${2:-}" == "--blocked" ]]; then cat "$BLOCKED_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true; exit 0; fi
    cat "$STATE_DIR/all_devices.txt" 2>/dev/null || printf '1: allow id 1d6b:0002\n2: block id 1234:5678\n'
    ;;
  list-rules) cat "$RULES_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true ;;
  allow-device) exit 0 ;;
  block-device) echo "$2: block" >> "$BLOCKED_FILE"; exit 0 ;;
  append-rule)
    rule=""; for arg in "$@"; do [[ "$arg" != "append-rule" && "$arg" != "--temporary" ]] && rule="$arg"; done
    max_id=$(awk -F: '{gsub(/ /,"",$1); if($1>max) max=$1} END{print max+0}' "$RULES_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
    new_id=$((max_id+1))
    printf '%s: %s\n' "$new_id" "$rule" >> "$RULES_FILE"
    printf '%s\n' "$new_id"
    exit 0
    ;;
  remove-rule)
    rid="${2:-}"; grep -v "^[[:space:]]*$rid:" "$RULES_FILE" > "$RULES_FILE.tmp" || true; mv "$RULES_FILE.tmp" "$RULES_FILE"; exit 0 ;;
  *) exit 64 ;;
esac
MOCK

create_ttl() {
  local mid="$1" rule="$2" hash="$3" expires="$4" status="$5" cid="$6"
  jq -n \
    --arg mid "$mid" \
    --arg rule "$rule" \
    --arg hash "$hash" \
    --arg status "$status" \
    --arg cid "$cid" \
    --argjson expires "$expires" \
    '{
      schema_version: 1,
      records: [{
        managed_id: $mid,
        rule: $rule,
        rule_sha256: $hash,
        expires_at: $expires,
        status: $status,
        correlation_id: $cid
      }]
    }'
}

# Create ttl.json with one expired active record
TTL="$TEST_DIR/state/ttl.json"

MID="11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111"
CID="22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222"
EXPIRED_EPOCH=$(( $(date +%s) - 100 ))  # already expired

create_ttl "$MID" "$RULE" "$HASH" "$EXPIRED_EPOCH" active "$CID" > "$TTL"

# Add rule to fake active rules
printf '10: %s\n' "$RULE" >> "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"

[[ $RC -eq 0 ]] || fail "cleanup-expired failed with $RC, log: $(cat $TEST_DIR/cleanup.log)"

# Verify record now expired

pass "cleanup-expired removes expired rule and marks expired"

# Verify rule removed from active rules
if grep -Fq "$RULE" "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"; then
  fail "rule should be removed from active rules after expire"
fi
pass "cleanup-expired verifies removal from active rules"

# Test reconcile: pending -> recovery_required

grep -Fq "$UNTRUSTED_RULE" "$FAKE_RULES_FILE" ||

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
# shellcheck source=../../scripts/lib/config-reader.sh
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/config-reader.sh"

if env CONFIG_READER_LOADED=1 bash -c \
  'source "$1"; [[ "$CONFIG_READER_VERSION" == "1.2.0" ]]' _ \
  "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/config-reader.sh"; then
  printf 'PASS: exported initialization marker cannot bypass setup\n'
else
  printf 'FAIL: exported initialization marker bypassed setup\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi

TEST_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$TEST_DIR"' EXIT

pass_count=0
fail_count=0

fail() {
  printf 'FAIL: %s\n' "$1" >&2
  fail_count=$((fail_count + 1))
}

pass() {
  printf 'PASS: %s\n' "$1"
  pass_count=$((pass_count + 1))
}

assert_config_value() {
  local key="$1"
  local expected="$2"
  local actual
  actual="$(get_config "$key")" || {
    fail "get_config $key"
    return
  }
  if [[ "$actual" == "$expected" ]]; then
    pass "get_config $key=$expected"
  else
    fail "get_config $key expected '$expected', got '$actual'"
  fi
}

assert_rejected() {
  local name="$1"
  local contents="$2"
  printf '%s' "$contents" > "$TEST_DIR/config"
  if load_config "$TEST_DIR/config" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    fail "$name was accepted"
  else
    pass "$name was rejected"
  fi
}

if load_config "$PROJECT_ROOT/conf/approval-manager.conf.example"; then
  pass "approved configuration example"
  assert_config_value TEMP_TTL_SECONDS 3600
  assert_config_value MIN_REASONABLE_EPOCH 1577836800
  assert_config_value MAX_CLOCK_JUMP_SECONDS 3600
  assert_config_value BACKUP_KEEP 5
  assert_config_value IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS 5
  assert_config_value LOG_LEVEL INFO
else
  fail "approved configuration example"
fi

cat > "$TEST_DIR/config" <<'EOF'
# Valid SRS v4.5 configuration
TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=00080
MAX_CLOCK_JUMP_SECONDS=86400
BACKUP_KEEP=100
IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=60
LOG_LEVEL=DEBUG
EOF
if load_config "$TEST_DIR/config"; then
  pass "valid configuration"
  assert_config_value TEMP_TTL_SECONDS 80
  assert_config_value MAX_CLOCK_JUMP_SECONDS 86400
  assert_config_value MIN_REASONABLE_EPOCH 1577836800
  assert_config_value BACKUP_KEEP 100
  assert_config_value IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS 60
  assert_config_value LOG_LEVEL DEBUG
else
  fail "valid configuration"
fi

for level in DEBUG INFO WARNING ERROR; do
  printf 'LOG_LEVEL=%s\n' "$level" > "$TEST_DIR/config"
  if load_config "$TEST_DIR/config" &&
    [[ "$(get_config LOG_LEVEL)" == "$level" ]]; then
    pass "accepted LOG_LEVEL=$level"
  else
    fail "rejected valid LOG_LEVEL=$level"
  fi
done

assert_rejected "duplicate key" \
  $'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=3600\nTEMP_TTL_SECONDS=7200\n'
assert_rejected "unknown key" $'UNLISTED_KEY=value\n'
assert_rejected "invalid log level" $'LOG_LEVEL=TRACE\n'
assert_rejected "out-of-range value" $'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=2592001\n'
assert_rejected "backup retention below minimum" $'BACKUP_KEEP=0\n'
assert_rejected "backup retention above maximum" $'BACKUP_KEEP=101\n'
assert_rejected "IPC timeout below minimum" $'IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=0\n'
assert_rejected "IPC timeout above maximum" $'IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=61\n'
assert_rejected "oversized out-of-range value" \
  $'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=999999999999999999999999999999999999999\n'
assert_rejected "command substitution" \
  "TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=\$(touch $TEST_DIR/injected)"
if [[ -e "$TEST_DIR/injected" ]]; then
  fail "command substitution was executed"
else
  pass "command substitution was not executed"
fi
assert_rejected "parameter expansion" 'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=${HOME}'
assert_rejected "backtick substitution" 'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=`id`'
assert_rejected "malformed assignment" $'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS\n'
assert_rejected "control character" $'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=3600\t\n'

printf '%s\n' 'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=3600' > "$TEST_DIR/config"
load_config "$TEST_DIR/config"
printf '%s\n' 'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=1' > "$TEST_DIR/config"
if load_config "$TEST_DIR/config" >/dev/null 2>&1 ||
  get_config TEMP_TTL_SECONDS >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  fail "failed load left stale configuration values"
else
  pass "failed load clears previously loaded configuration"
fi

printf 'TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=3600\0\n' > "$TEST_DIR/config"
if load_config "$TEST_DIR/config" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  fail "NUL byte was accepted"
else
  pass "NUL byte was rejected"
fi

if [[ "$fail_count" -ne 0 ]]; then
  printf '%s test(s) failed; %s passed\n' "$fail_count" "$pass_count" >&2
  exit 1
fi
printf 'All %s config-reader checks passed\n' "$pass_count"

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
PREFLIGHT="$PROJECT_ROOT/tests/integration/collect_g01_readonly_preflight.sh"
TEST_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
FAKE_BIN="$TEST_DIR/bin"
mkdir -p "$FAKE_BIN" "$TEST_DIR/rules.d"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$TEST_DIR"' EXIT

fail() {
  printf 'FAIL: %s\n' "$1" >&2
  exit 1
}

pass() {
  printf 'PASS: %s\n' "$1"
}

cat > "$FAKE_BIN/usbguard" <<'MOCK'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
printf '%s\n' "$*" >> "$FAKE_USB_COMMANDS"
case "$*" in
  --version)
    printf 'USBGuard 1.1.4\n'
    ;;
  'list-devices')
    printf '1: allow id 1d6b:0002 name "Lab device"\n'
    ;;
  'list-devices --blocked')
    printf '3: block id 1234:5678 name "Test device"\n'
    ;;
  list-rules)
    printf '1: block id 1234:5678\n'
    ;;
  *)
    printf 'forbidden or unexpected USBGuard command: %s\n' "$*" >&2
    exit 64
    ;;
esac
MOCK

cat > "$FAKE_BIN/pgrep" <<'MOCK'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
[[ "$*" == "-x usbguard-daemon" ]] || exit 64
printf '42\n'
MOCK

chmod 700 "$FAKE_BIN/usbguard" "$FAKE_BIN/pgrep"
export PATH="$FAKE_BIN:$PATH"
FAKE_USB_COMMANDS="$TEST_DIR/commands.log"
export FAKE_USB_COMMANDS
IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=1
export IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
POLICY_FILE="$TEST_DIR/rules.conf"
touch "$POLICY_FILE"
DAEMON_CONFIG="$TEST_DIR/usbguard-daemon.conf"
printf 'RuleFile=%s\nRuleFolder=%s\n' "$POLICY_FILE" "$TEST_DIR/rules.d" \
  > "$DAEMON_CONFIG"
USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH="$DAEMON_CONFIG"
export USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH

set +e
output="$(env -u USBAM_G01_EXPECTED_HOSTNAME bash "$PREFLIGHT" 2>&1)"
status=$?
set -e
[[ "$status" -eq 9 ]] || fail "missing expected hostname did not fail closed"
[[ "$output" == *"T01_RESULT=UNVERIFIED"* ]] ||
  fail "host mismatch did not report UNVERIFIED"
[[ ! -s "$FAKE_USB_COMMANDS" ]] ||
  fail "USBGuard was queried before the hostname gate"
pass "expected-hostname gate prevents queries on an unconfirmed host"

export USBAM_G01_EXPECTED_HOSTNAME
USBAM_G01_EXPECTED_HOSTNAME="$(hostname)"
set +e
output="$(bash "$PREFLIGHT" 2>&1)"
status=$?
set -e
[[ "$status" -eq 9 ]] || fail "preflight did not keep G-01 gated"
[[ "$output" == *"T01_RESULT=READ_ONLY_EVIDENCE_COLLECTED"* ]] ||
  fail "successful read-only collection was not reported"
[[ "$output" == *"G01_STATUS=UNVERIFIED"* ]] ||
  fail "preflight incorrectly reported G-01 as verified"
[[ "$output" == *"POLICY_CHANGING_TESTS=NOT_RUN"* ]] ||
  fail "preflight did not report that policy-changing tests were skipped"
[[ "$output" == *"RuleFile="* && "$output" == *"RuleFolder="* ]] ||
  fail "both configured policy source paths were not reported"
expected_commands=$'--version\nlist-devices\nlist-devices --blocked\nlist-rules'
asserted_commands="$(<"$FAKE_USB_COMMANDS")"
[[ "$asserted_commands" == "$expected_commands" ]] ||
  fail "preflight ran a command outside its read-only allowlist"
pass "preflight gathers read-only evidence and leaves G-01 unverified"

import hashlib
import io
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import tarfile
import tempfile
import unittest
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
HELPER_PATH = ROOT / "scripts" / "lib" / "lifecycle.py"
SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("lifecycle", HELPER_PATH)
assert SPEC is not None and SPEC.loader is not None
lifecycle = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
SPEC.loader.exec_module(lifecycle)


def ttl_record(rule: str, status: str = "active") -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "managed_id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
        "rule": rule,
        "rule_sha256": hashlib.sha256(rule.encode()).hexdigest(),
        "expires_at": 2_000_000_000,
        "status": status,
        "correlation_id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
    }


class LifecycleTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="usbam-lifecycle-")
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.config = self.root / "approval-manager.conf"
        self.ttl = self.root / "ttl.json"
        self.export = self.root / "export.json"
        self.config.write_text("BACKUP_KEEP=5\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.ttl.write_text(
            json.dumps({"schema_version": 1, "records": [ttl_record("allow id 1234:5678")]})
            + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        self.export.write_text(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "schema_version": 1,
                    "permanent_rules": ["allow id 1234:5678"],
                    "ttl_records": [
                        {"rule": "allow id abcd:ef01", "expires_at": 2_000_000_000}
                    ],
                }
            )
            + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        self.backup_dir = self.root / "backups"
        self.backup_dir.mkdir(mode=0o700)

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def test_backup_round_trip_has_closed_manifest_and_payloads(self) -> None:
        archive = lifecycle.create_backup(
            self.config, self.ttl, self.export, self.backup_dir, keep=2
        )

        payloads = lifecycle.read_backup(archive)

        self.assertEqual(set(payloads), lifecycle.PAYLOAD_PATHS)
        extracted = self.root / "extracted"
        lifecycle.extract_backup(archive, extracted)
        for name, data in payloads.items():
            target = extracted / name
            self.assertEqual(target.read_bytes(), data)
            self.assertEqual(target.stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o600)
        with tarfile.open(archive, "r:gz") as archive_file:
            manifest_member = archive_file.extractfile("manifest.json")
            self.assertIsNotNone(manifest_member)
            manifest = json.loads(manifest_member.read())
        self.assertEqual(
            {entry["path"] for entry in manifest["entries"]}, lifecycle.PAYLOAD_PATHS
        )
        self.assertTrue(all(entry["mode"] == 0o600 for entry in manifest["entries"]))

    def test_import_rejects_system_data_extra_fields_and_duplicate_rules(self) -> None:
        for document in (
            {
                "schema_version": 1,
                "permanent_rules": ["allow id 1234:5678", "allow id 1234:5678"],
                "ttl_records": [],
            },
            {
                "schema_version": 1,
                "permanent_rules": ["allow id 1234:5678"],
                "ttl_records": [],
                "system": [],
            },
            {
                "schema_version": 1,
                "permanent_rules": ["allow id 1234:5678"],
                "ttl_records": [
                    {"rule": "allow id 1234:5678 00-system.rules", "expires_at": 2_000_000_000}
                ],
            },
        ):
            with self.subTest(document=document), self.assertRaises(lifecycle.LifecycleError):
                lifecycle.validate_export(document)

    def test_export_enforces_rule_count_and_expiry_bounds(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(lifecycle.LifecycleError, "too many rules"):
            lifecycle.validate_export(
                {
                    "schema_version": 1,
                    "permanent_rules": ["allow id 1234:5678"] * 10001,
                    "ttl_records": [],
                }
            )
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(lifecycle.LifecycleError, "expires_at"):
            lifecycle.validate_export(
                {
                    "schema_version": 1,
                    "permanent_rules": [],
                    "ttl_records": [
                        {"rule": "allow id 1234:5678", "expires_at": 253402300800}
                    ],
                }
            )

    def test_archive_rejects_traversal_and_hash_mismatch(self) -> None:
        archive = lifecycle.create_backup(
            self.config, self.ttl, self.export, self.backup_dir, keep=2
        )
        traversal = self.root / "traversal.tar.gz"
        with tarfile.open(archive, "r:gz") as source, tarfile.open(
            traversal, "w:gz"
        ) as target:
            for member in source.getmembers():
                data = source.extractfile(member).read()
                member.name = "../" + member.name
                target.addfile(member, io.BytesIO(data))
        with self.assertRaises(lifecycle.LifecycleError):
            lifecycle.read_backup(traversal)

        altered = self.root / "altered.tar.gz"
        with tarfile.open(archive, "r:gz") as source, tarfile.open(altered, "w:gz") as target:
            for member in source.getmembers():
                data = source.extractfile(member).read()
                if member.name == "policy/export.json":
                    data = bytes([data[0] ^ 1]) + data[1:]
                target.addfile(member, io.BytesIO(data))
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(lifecycle.LifecycleError, "integrity"):
            lifecycle.read_backup(altered)

    def _restore_fixture(self) -> tuple[Path, Path, Path, Path, Path]:
        stage = self.root / "stage"
        (stage / "config").mkdir(parents=True)
        (stage / "state").mkdir()
        (stage / "policy").mkdir()
        (stage / "config" / "approval-manager.conf").write_text(
            "BACKUP_KEEP=7\n", encoding="utf-8"
        )
        (stage / "state" / "ttl.json").write_text(
            json.dumps({"schema_version": 1, "records": [ttl_record("allow id 2222:3333")]})
            + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        (stage / "policy" / "rules.conf").write_text("allow id 1234:5678\n", encoding="utf-8")
        targets = {
            "config": str(self.root / "target.conf"),
            "ttl": str(self.root / "target-ttl.json"),
            "policy": str(self.root / "target-rules.conf"),
        }
        for path in targets.values():
            Path(path).write_text("old\n", encoding="utf-8")
        targets_file = self.root / "targets.json"
        targets_file.write_text(json.dumps(targets), encoding="utf-8")
        journal = self.root / "state" / "restore_journal.json"
        journal.parent.mkdir()
        return stage, targets_file, journal, self.root / "rollback", self.root

    def test_restore_atomically_recovers_ttl_records_as_recovery_required(self) -> None:
        stage, targets_file, journal, rollback_root, _ = self._restore_fixture()

        lifecycle.apply_restore(journal, rollback_root, targets_file, stage)

        self.assertFalse(journal.exists())
        targets = json.loads(targets_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        restored_ttl = json.loads(Path(targets["ttl"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(restored_ttl["records"][0]["status"], "recovery_required")
        self.assertEqual(Path(targets["policy"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"), "allow id 1234:5678\n")

    def test_recovery_can_be_retried_after_journal_cleanup_failure(self) -> None:
        stage, targets_file, journal, rollback_root, _ = self._restore_fixture()
        with mock.patch.object(
            lifecycle, "_remove_journal", side_effect=lifecycle.LifecycleError("simulated crash")
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(lifecycle.LifecycleError):
                lifecycle.apply_restore(journal, rollback_root, targets_file, stage)
        self.assertTrue(journal.exists())

        with mock.patch.object(
            lifecycle, "_remove_journal", side_effect=lifecycle.LifecycleError("simulated crash")
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(lifecycle.LifecycleError):
                lifecycle.recover_restore(journal, rollback_root, targets_file)
        lifecycle.recover_restore(journal, rollback_root, targets_file)

        self.assertFalse(journal.exists())
        targets = json.loads(targets_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertTrue(all(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "old\n" for path in targets.values()))

    def test_failed_recovery_keeps_journal_marked_for_recovery(self) -> None:
        stage, targets_file, journal, rollback_root, _ = self._restore_fixture()
        failure = lifecycle.LifecycleError("simulated rollback failure")
        with (
            mock.patch.object(lifecycle, "_remove_journal", side_effect=failure),
            mock.patch.object(lifecycle, "_rollback_transaction", side_effect=failure),
            self.assertRaises(lifecycle.LifecycleError),
        ):
            lifecycle.apply_restore(journal, rollback_root, targets_file, stage)
        self.assertEqual(
            json.loads(journal.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["status"], "recovery_required"
        )

        with (
            mock.patch.object(lifecycle, "_rollback_transaction", side_effect=failure),
            self.assertRaisesRegex(lifecycle.LifecycleError, "recovery is incomplete"),
        ):
            lifecycle.recover_restore(journal, rollback_root, targets_file)
        self.assertEqual(
            json.loads(journal.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["status"], "recovery_required"
        )

    @unittest.skipUnless(hasattr(os, "symlink"), "symlinks are unavailable")
    def test_backup_refuses_symbolic_link(self) -> None:
        link = self.root / "linked-export.json"
        link.symlink_to(self.export)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(lifecycle.LifecycleError, "safely open"):
            lifecycle.create_backup(self.config, self.ttl, link, self.backup_dir, keep=2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
LOCK_LIBRARY="$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/lock.sh"
TEST_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
LOCK_PATH="$TEST_DIR/manager.lock"
touch "$LOCK_PATH"
chmod 600 "$LOCK_PATH"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$TEST_DIR"' EXIT

fail() {
  printf 'FAIL: %s\n' "$1" >&2
  exit 1
}

pass() {
  printf 'PASS: %s\n' "$1"
}

bash -c '
  source "$1"
  LOCK_FILE="$2"
  lock_acquire 1
  [[ "$LOCK_HELD" == 1 ]]
  lock_release
  [[ "$LOCK_HELD" == 0 ]]
' _ "$LOCK_LIBRARY" "$LOCK_PATH" ||
  fail "free lock acquisition and explicit release"
pass "free lock acquisition and explicit release"

READY="$TEST_DIR/ready"
bash -c '
  source "$1"
  LOCK_FILE="$2"
  lock_acquire 1
  : > "$3"
  sleep 0.6
' _ "$LOCK_LIBRARY" "$LOCK_PATH" "$READY" &
HOLDER_PID=$!
for _ in {1..100}; do
  [[ -e "$READY" ]] && break
  sleep 0.01
done
[[ -e "$READY" ]] || fail "lock holder did not start"
if bash -c '
  source "$1"
  LOCK_FILE="$2"
  lock_acquire 0.1
' _ "$LOCK_LIBRARY" "$LOCK_PATH" 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "second process acquired a held lock"
fi
wait "$HOLDER_PID" || fail "lock holder failed"
pass "competing process times out safely"

bash -c '
  source "$1"
  LOCK_FILE="$2"
  lock_acquire 1
' _ "$LOCK_LIBRARY" "$LOCK_PATH" ||
  fail "EXIT trap did not release lock"
pass "EXIT trap releases lock"

if bash -c '
  source "$1"
  LOCK_FILE="$2"
  trap ":" EXIT
  lock_acquire 1
' _ "$LOCK_LIBRARY" "$LOCK_PATH" 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "lock acquisition replaced a caller EXIT trap"
fi
pass "existing EXIT trap is preserved and acquisition fails closed"

bash -c '
  source "$1"
  LOCK_FILE="$2"
  trap "lock_release; :" EXIT
  lock_acquire 1
  [[ "$LOCK_HELD" == 1 ]]
' _ "$LOCK_LIBRARY" "$LOCK_PATH" ||
  fail "composed EXIT trap was rejected"
pass "composed caller EXIT trap retains lock release"

if bash -c '
  source "$1"
  LOCK_FILE="$2"
  trap ":; lock_release" EXIT
  lock_acquire 1
' _ "$LOCK_LIBRARY" "$LOCK_PATH" 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "EXIT trap with cleanup before lock release was accepted"
fi
pass "unsafe EXIT trap ordering is rejected"

if bash -c '
  source "$1"
  LOCK_FILE="$2"
  lock_acquire -1
' _ "$LOCK_LIBRARY" "$LOCK_PATH" 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "negative timeout was accepted"
fi
pass "invalid timeout is rejected"

LINK_PATH="$TEST_DIR/linked.lock"
ln -s "$LOCK_PATH" "$LINK_PATH"
if bash -c '
  source "$1"
  LOCK_FILE="$2"
  lock_acquire 1
' _ "$LOCK_LIBRARY" "$LINK_PATH" 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "symbolic-link lock path was accepted"
fi
pass "symbolic-link lock path is rejected"

printf 'All lock checks passed\n'

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
LOGGER_LIBRARY="$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/logger.sh"
TEST_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$TEST_DIR"' EXIT

mkdir "$TEST_DIR/bin"
cat > "$TEST_DIR/bin/logger" <<'LOGGER'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
printf '%s\n' "$*" >> "$LOGGER_CAPTURE"
LOGGER
chmod 700 "$TEST_DIR/bin/logger"

# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# בדיקת יחידה לספריית לוגים (logger).
# בודקת הפרדה בין לוג תפעולי syslog לזרם Audit JSONL מוגן.
# בודקת כתיבת הודעות info/error דרך פקודת logger.
# בודקת כתיבת Audit JSONL דרך audit_write.py עם הרשאות מתאימות.
# מאמתת שכתיבת Audit שנכשלה מדווחת כיאות ושומרת תוצאת מדיניות.
export LOGGER_CAPTURE="$TEST_DIR/operational.log"
export PATH="$TEST_DIR/bin:$PATH"
source "$LOGGER_LIBRARY"
source "$LOGGER_LIBRARY"

fail() {
  printf 'FAIL: %s\n' "$1" >&2
  exit 1
}

pass() {
  printf 'PASS: %s\n' "$1"
}

log_info "started safely" || fail "informational log call"
log_error "operational failure" || fail "error log call"
if log_info $'forged\nrecord' 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "control character was accepted in operational log"
fi
[[ "$(wc -l < "$LOGGER_CAPTURE")" -eq 2 ]] ||
  fail "operational logging did not produce exactly two safe calls"
grep -Fq 'started safely' "$LOGGER_CAPTURE" ||
  fail "operational message was not passed to system logger"
pass "operational logging uses system logger and rejects control characters"

if log_audit approve applied id details extra 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "invalid Audit argument count was accepted"
fi
pass "invalid Audit argument count is rejected"

if log_audit approve applied id "safe details" 2>"$TEST_DIR/audit-error.log"; then
  fail "unavailable protected Audit destination reported success"
fi
grep -Fq 'Audit write failed' "$TEST_DIR/audit-error.log" ||
  fail "Audit write failure was not reported"
pass "Audit write failure is explicit"

printf 'All logger checks passed\n'

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/config-reader.sh"
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/usbguard-policy.sh"

TEST_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
FAKE_BIN="$TEST_DIR/bin"
mkdir -p "$FAKE_BIN"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$TEST_DIR"' EXIT

cat > "$FAKE_BIN/usbguard" <<'MOCK'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
printf '%s\n' "$*" >> "$FAKE_USB_COMMANDS"
case "$*" in
  --version)
    printf 'USBGuard 1.1.4\n'
    ;;
  'list-devices --blocked')
    case "${FAKE_DEVICE_MODE:-normal}" in
      empty) exit 0 ;;
      malformed) printf 'not a USBGuard record\n' ;;
      failure)
        printf 'query failed\n' >&2
        exit 3
        ;;
      slow) sleep 5 ;;
      *)
        printf '3: block id 1d6b:0002 name "Lab device"\n'
        printf '4: block id 1234:5678 name "Second lab device"\n'
        ;;
    esac
    ;;
  list-devices)
    if [[ "${FAKE_IPC_MODE:-connected}" == "unavailable" ]]; then
      printf 'IPC unavailable\n' >&2
      exit 4
    fi
    printf '1: allow id 1d6b:0002 name "Lab device"\n'
    ;;
  list-rules)
    if [[ "${FAKE_RULE_MODE:-normal}" == "empty" ]]; then
      exit 0
    fi
    printf '1: allow id 1d6b:0002\n'
    printf '2: block id 9999:0001\n'
    ;;
  *)
    printf 'unexpected command\n' >&2
    exit 64
    ;;
esac
MOCK

cat > "$FAKE_BIN/pgrep" <<'MOCK'

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/usbguard-policy.sh"

TEST_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
FAKE_BIN="$TEST_DIR/bin"
mkdir -p "$FAKE_BIN"
trap 'lock_release; rm -rf -- "$TEST_DIR"' EXIT

FAKE_RULES_FILE="$TEST_DIR/active_rules.txt"
printf '1: allow id 1d6b:0002\n2: block id 9999:0001\n' > "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"
FAKE_DEVICES_FILE="$TEST_DIR/devices.txt"
printf '1: allow id 1d6b:0002\n3: block id 1234:5678\n4: block id abcd:ef01\n5: allow id 1111:2222\n' > "$FAKE_DEVICES_FILE"

cat > "$FAKE_BIN/usbguard" <<'MOCK'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
STATE_DIR="${FAKE_STATE_DIR:?}"
RULES_FILE="$STATE_DIR/active_rules.txt"
DEVICES_FILE="$STATE_DIR/devices.txt"
LOG_FILE="$STATE_DIR/usbguard-commands.log"
printf '%s\n' "$*" >> "$LOG_FILE"

case "$1" in
  --version)
    printf 'USBGuard 1.1.4\n'
    ;;
  list-devices)
    if [[ "${2:-}" == "--blocked" ]]; then
      grep -E '^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+: block([[:space:]]|$)' "$DEVICES_FILE" || true
    else
      cat "$DEVICES_FILE"
    fi
    ;;
  list-rules)
    cat "$RULES_FILE"
    ;;
  allow-device|block-device)
    dev_id="${2:-}"
    if [[ ! "$dev_id" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
      echo "invalid device id" >&2
      exit 2
    fi
    if [[ "$1" == "allow-device" && "${FAKE_ALLOW_NOOP:-0}" == "1" ]] ||
      [[ "$1" == "block-device" && "${FAKE_BLOCK_NOOP:-0}" == "1" ]]; then
      exit 0
    fi
    if ! grep -qE "^[[:space:]]*$dev_id:" "$DEVICES_FILE"; then
      echo "device not found" >&2
      exit 7
    fi
    if [[ "$1" == "allow-device" ]]; then
      sed -E "s/^$dev_id: (block|reject)/$dev_id: allow/" "$DEVICES_FILE" > "$DEVICES_FILE.tmp"
    else
      sed -E "s/^$dev_id: allow/$dev_id: block/" "$DEVICES_FILE" > "$DEVICES_FILE.tmp"
    fi
    mv "$DEVICES_FILE.tmp" "$DEVICES_FILE"
    ;;
  append-rule)
    rule=""
    for arg in "$@"; do
      [[ "$arg" == "append-rule" || "$arg" == "--temporary" ]] || rule="$arg"
    done
    if [[ "$rule" == *"00-system.rules"* ]]; then
      echo "protected" >&2
      exit 5
    fi
    if grep -Fq "$rule" "$RULES_FILE"; then
      echo "duplicate" >&2
      exit 6
    fi
    max_id="$(awk -F: '{gsub(/ /,"",$1); if($1>max) max=$1} END{print max+0}' "$RULES_FILE")"
    new_id=$((max_id + 1))
    printf '%s: %s\n' "$new_id" "$rule" >> "$RULES_FILE"
    if [[ " $* " != *" --temporary "* ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$rule" >> "${FAKE_PERSISTENT_RULES_FILE:?}"
    fi
    printf '%s\n' "$new_id"
    ;;
  remove-rule)
    rid="${2:-}"
    if [[ ! "$rid" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || ! grep -qE "^[[:space:]]*$rid:" "$RULES_FILE"; then
      echo "rule not found" >&2
      exit 4
    fi
    grep -vE "^[[:space:]]*$rid:" "$RULES_FILE" > "$RULES_FILE.tmp" || true
    mv "$RULES_FILE.tmp" "$RULES_FILE"
    ;;
  *)
    echo "unexpected command: $*" >&2
    exit 64
    ;;
esac
MOCK

cat > "$FAKE_BIN/pgrep" <<'MOCK'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
if [[ "${FAKE_DAEMON_MODE:-running}" == "running" ]]; then
  printf '42\n'
  exit 0
fi
exit 1
MOCK

chmod 700 "$FAKE_BIN/usbguard" "$FAKE_BIN/pgrep"
PATH="$FAKE_BIN:$PATH"
export PATH FAKE_STATE_DIR="$TEST_DIR"
IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=1
USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH="$TEST_DIR/usbguard-daemon.conf"
printf 'RuleFile=%s\n' "$TEST_DIR/rules.conf" > "$USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH"
touch "$TEST_DIR/rules.conf"
FAKE_PERSISTENT_RULES_FILE="$TEST_DIR/rules.conf"
export FAKE_PERSISTENT_RULES_FILE

fail() {
  printf 'FAIL: %s\n' "$1" >&2
  exit 1
}
pass() {
  printf 'PASS: %s\n' "$1"
}

# Verify that legacy environment and /tmp-based bypasses are not accepted.
unset USBAM_G01_VERIFIED USBAM_ALLOW_BLOCK
if ! is_g01_verified; then
  export USBAM_G01_VERIFIED=1 USBAM_ALLOW_BLOCK=1
  if is_g01_verified; then
    fail "environment variables bypassed the G-01 marker"
  fi
  pass "G-01 environment overrides are ignored"
else
  pass "G-01 marker is already valid on this test host"
fi
unset USBAM_G01_VERIFIED USBAM_ALLOW_BLOCK

# Test canonicalization and hashing.
canon="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule '  allow id 1234:5678  ')"
[[ "$canon" == "allow id 1234:5678" ]] || fail "canonicalize trim failed: '$canon'"
pass "canonicalize trims whitespace"

hash1="$(usbguard_hash_rule 'allow id 1234:5678')"
hash2="$(usbguard_hash_rule 'allow id 1234:5678')"
[[ "$hash1" == "$hash2" && "${#hash1}" -eq 64 ]] || fail "hash is not stable SHA-256"
pass "hash is deterministic SHA-256"

# The fake gate is local to this test process; production verification remains
# based exclusively on the root-owned evidence marker.
is_g01_verified() {
  [[ "${FAKE_G01_VERIFIED:-0}" == "1" ]]
}

FAKE_G01_VERIFIED=0
if usbguard_policy_run_write block-device 5 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "write runner accepted a mutation before G-01"
fi
[[ "$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" == "g01_not_verified" ]] || fail "write gate error code mismatch"
FAKE_G01_VERIFIED=1
if usbguard_policy_run_write block-device 5 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "write runner accepted a mutation without the global lock"
fi
[[ "$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" == "lock_not_held" ]] || fail "global-lock error code mismatch"
LOCK_FILE="$TEST_DIR/policy.lock"
: > "$LOCK_FILE"
chmod 600 "$LOCK_FILE"
lock_acquire 1 || fail "could not acquire the test lock"
pass "all write operations require G-01 verification and the global lock"

# Missing arguments must be reported, not expanded under nounset.
for command_name in approve_device add_temporary_rule add_permanent_rule \
  remove_rule_by_canonical_rule block_device; do
  if "$command_name" 2>/dev/null; then
    fail "$command_name accepted missing arguments"
  fi
  [[ "$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" == "invalid_arguments" ]] || fail "$command_name missing-argument error code mismatch"
done
pass "write operations reject missing arguments safely"

# Approvals must target a live device and verify its resulting state.
list_blocked_devices >/dev/null || fail "initial blocked list failed"
[[ "${#USBAM_BLOCKED_DEVICES[@]}" -eq 2 ]] || fail "initial blocked count mismatch"
approve_device 3 || fail "approve_device 3 failed"
[[ "$USBAM_LAST_DEVICE_ID" == "3" ]] || fail "last device ID not set"
if grep -qE '^3: block([[:space:]]|$)' "$FAKE_DEVICES_FILE"; then
  fail "device 3 remains blocked after approval"
fi
pass "approve_device verifies the selected device is allowed"

printf '3: block id 1234:5678\n' > "$TEST_DIR/device-restore"
grep -vE '^3:' "$FAKE_DEVICES_FILE" >> "$TEST_DIR/device-restore"
mv "$TEST_DIR/device-restore" "$FAKE_DEVICES_FILE"
export FAKE_ALLOW_NOOP=1
if approve_device 3 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "approve_device accepted a successful command with unchanged blocked state"
fi
[[ "$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" == "device_still_blocked" ]] || fail "approval verification error code mismatch"
unset FAKE_ALLOW_NOOP
pass "approve_device fails when USBGuard does not change device state"

if approve_device "abc" 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "approve_device accepted an invalid ID"
fi
pass "approve_device rejects invalid IDs"

# Temporary and permanent rule validation.
add_temporary_rule 'allow id abcd:1234 serial "TEST"' || fail "temporary rule append failed"
[[ -n "$USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID" ]] || fail "temporary rule ID not set"
pass "temporary rule append succeeds"

if add_temporary_rule 'allow id abcd:1234 serial "TEST"' 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "duplicate temporary rule was accepted"
fi
pass "duplicate temporary rules are rejected"

if add_temporary_rule 'allow id 9999:0001' 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "block-before-allow conflict was accepted"
fi
[[ "$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" == "precedence_conflict" ]] || fail "precedence conflict error code mismatch"
pass "block-before-allow conflict is rejected"

printf '98: block with-interface equals {03:*:*}\n' >> "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"
if add_temporary_rule 'allow id 1234:5678' 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "generic block-before-specific-allow conflict was accepted"
fi
[[ "$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" == "precedence_conflict" ]] || fail "generic precedence error code mismatch"
grep -vE '^98:' "$FAKE_RULES_FILE" > "$FAKE_RULES_FILE.tmp"
mv "$FAKE_RULES_FILE.tmp" "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"
pass "generic earlier blocks conservatively reject later allows"

if add_temporary_rule 'allow id 1234:5678 path "00-system.rules"' 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "protected rule was accepted"
fi
pass "protected 00-system.rules is rejected"

add_permanent_rule 'allow id 1111:2222' || fail "permanent rule append failed"
grep -Fqx 'allow id 1111:2222' "$FAKE_PERSISTENT_RULES_FILE" ||
  fail "permanent rule was not verified in the persistent policy"
pass "permanent rule append succeeds"

usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical 'allow id 1111:2222' >/dev/null ||
  fail "existing canonical rule was not found"
[[ -n "$USBAM_MATCHED_RULE_ID" ]] || fail "matched rule ID is empty"
pass "canonical rule lookup finds a unique match"

printf '99: allow id 1111:2222\n' >> "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"
if usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical 'allow id 1111:2222' 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "ambiguous rule match was accepted"
fi
[[ "$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" == "ambiguous_rule_match" ]] || fail "ambiguous match error code mismatch"
grep -vE '^99:' "$FAKE_RULES_FILE" > "$FAKE_RULES_FILE.tmp"
mv "$FAKE_RULES_FILE.tmp" "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"
pass "canonical rule lookup rejects ambiguous matches"

remove_rule_by_canonical_rule 'allow id 1111:2222' || fail "canonical rule removal failed"
if usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical 'allow id 1111:2222' 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "removed rule remains active"
fi
pass "canonical rule removal is verified"

if remove_rule_by_canonical_rule 'allow id 9999:8888' 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "removal of a missing rule succeeded"
fi
pass "missing rules cannot be removed"

# Block operations must also verify the resulting state.
if block_device 99 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "block_device succeeded for a missing device"
fi
[[ "$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" == "command_failed" ]] || fail "missing-device block error code mismatch"

export FAKE_BLOCK_NOOP=1
if block_device 5 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "block_device accepted a successful command with unchanged state"
fi
[[ "$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" == "device_not_blocked" ]] || fail "block verification error code mismatch"
unset FAKE_BLOCK_NOOP

block_device 5 || fail "block_device failed for a live device"
grep -qE '^5: block([[:space:]]|$)' "$FAKE_DEVICES_FILE" || fail "device 5 is not blocked"
pass "block_device verifies the selected device is blocked"

if usbguard_policy_run_readonly allow-device 1 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "read-only runner accepted a write operation"
fi
pass "read-only runner rejects write operations"

printf 'All policy write checks passed\n'

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
# shellcheck source=../../scripts/lib/result.sh
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/result.sh"
set -o errexit -o nounset -o pipefail
# Sourcing a library twice must not reset results or fail on readonly constants.
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/result.sh"

# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# בדיקת יחידה ל-model תוצאות/שגיאות (result model).
# בודקת אתחול, קודי יציאה, מצבי תוצאה והדפסת JSON.
# מודל תוצאות משותף (SRS §21, SEC-16) עם קבועי יציאה מוגדרים מראש
# ופונקציות ניהול state גלובלי.
# בודקת שכפילת אתחול, תרגום מצבים ופלט JSON תקין.

if env RESULT_MODEL_LOADED=1 bash -c \
  'source "$1"; [[ "$EXIT_AUDIT" == "14" ]]' _ \
  "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/result.sh"; then
  printf 'PASS: exported initialization marker cannot bypass setup\n'
else
  printf 'FAIL: exported initialization marker bypassed setup\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi

TEST_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$TEST_DIR"' EXIT

fail() {
  printf 'FAIL: %s\n' "$1" >&2
  exit 1
}

assert_equal() {
  local expected="$1"
  local actual="$2"
  local name="$3"
  [[ "$actual" == "$expected" ]] || fail "$name: expected '$expected', got '$actual'"
  printf 'PASS: %s\n' "$name"
}

result_start
result_partial "one item succeeded; one failed"
assert_equal "$RESULT_PARTIAL" "$RESULT_STATE" "partial state is not success"
assert_equal "$EXIT_PARTIAL" "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE" "partial exit code"
assert_equal "unknown" "$RESULT_POLICY_STATUS" "partial policy aggregate is unknown"
assert_equal "not_applicable" "$RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS" "partial audit default is valid"
if result_set "INVALID" 8 "unsupported code" 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "unsupported exit code was accepted"
fi
if result_set "$RESULT_POLICY_APPLIED_AUDIT_FAILED" "$EXIT_OK" \
  "invalid state/code pairing" 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "mismatched result state and exit code were accepted"
fi
if result_partial "invalid partial status" partial partial 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "unsupported partial statuses were accepted"
fi
assert_equal "$RESULT_PARTIAL" "$RESULT_STATE" "invalid result leaves state unchanged"

result_start
result_partial "partial batch with known audit outcome" unknown written \
  "batch_policy_incomplete" ""
assert_equal "unknown" "$RESULT_POLICY_STATUS" "partial batch accepts valid policy status"
assert_equal "written" "$RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS" "partial batch accepts valid audit status"
assert_equal "batch_policy_incomplete" "$RESULT_POLICY_DETAIL_CODE" \
  "partial batch preserves policy detail code"

result_start
result_policy_applied "policy and audit verified"
assert_equal "applied" "$RESULT_POLICY_STATUS" "successful policy result"
assert_equal "written" "$RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS" "successful audit result"

result_start
result_operation_failed "precondition failed"
assert_equal "$EXIT_OPERATION" "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE" "operational failure exit code"
assert_equal "not_applied" "$RESULT_POLICY_STATUS" "operational failure policy result"

result_start
result_validation_failed "invalid input"
assert_equal "$EXIT_VALIDATION" "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE" "validation failure exit code"
assert_equal "$RESULT_VALIDATION_FAILED" "$RESULT_STATE" "validation failure state"

result_start
result_policy_failed "policy command rejected"
assert_equal "$EXIT_OPERATION" "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE" "known policy failure exit code"
assert_equal "not_applied" "$RESULT_POLICY_STATUS" "known policy failure outcome"

result_start
result_unknown "policy outcome cannot be verified"
assert_equal "$EXIT_INCONSISTENT" "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE" "unknown outcome exit code"
assert_equal "unknown" "$RESULT_POLICY_STATUS" "unknown policy outcome"

result_start
result_recovery_required "manual recovery is required"
assert_equal "$EXIT_INCONSISTENT" "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE" "recovery exit code"

result_start
result_policy_applied_audit_failed $'audit "failed"\nsecond line\tend; apostrophe: \047; Unicode: \u00e9; control: \001'
assert_equal "$EXIT_AUDIT" "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE" "audit failure exit code"
assert_equal "applied" "$RESULT_POLICY_STATUS" "policy outcome retained"
assert_equal "failed" "$RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS" "audit failure distinguished"
[[ "$RESULT_CORRELATION_ID" =~ ^[0-9a-f]{8}-([0-9a-f]{4}-){3}[0-9a-f]{12}$ ]] ||
  fail "correlation ID is not a UUID"

if result_add_item "invalid-device" "$RESULT_POLICY_FAILED" "$EXIT_OPERATION" \
  "contradictory status" applied written 2>/dev/null; then
  fail "per-item status contradicting its result state was accepted"
fi
assert_equal "0" "${#RESULT_ITEM_JSON[@]}" "invalid item is not appended"
result_add_item "device-01" "$RESULT_SUCCESS" "$EXIT_OK" "approved" \
  applied written
result_add_item "device-02" "$RESULT_POLICY_FAILED" "$EXIT_OPERATION" \
  "policy rejected" not_applied not_applicable "policy_rejected"
result_print_json > "$TEST_DIR/result.json"
python3 -m json.tool "$TEST_DIR/result.json" >/dev/null ||
  fail "result JSON is invalid"
assert_equal "applied" "$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["policy_result"]["status"])' "$TEST_DIR/result.json")" \
  "JSON contains separate policy result"
assert_equal "True" "$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["policy_result"]["detail_code"] is None)' "$TEST_DIR/result.json")" \
  "policy result includes nullable detail code"
assert_equal "failed" "$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["audit_result"]["status"])' "$TEST_DIR/result.json")" \
  "JSON contains separate audit result"
assert_equal "policy_rejected" "$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["items"][1]["policy_result"]["detail_code"])' "$TEST_DIR/result.json")" \
  "batch item preserves its policy detail code"
assert_equal "2" "$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(len(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["items"]))' "$TEST_DIR/result.json")" \
  "JSON contains a result for each batch item"
assert_equal $'audit "failed"\nsecond line\tend; apostrophe: \047; Unicode: \u00e9; control: \001' \
  "$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["detail"], end="")' "$TEST_DIR/result.json")" \
  "JSON detail round-trip"

result_start
result_cancelled "cancelled before policy change"
assert_equal "$EXIT_CANCELLED" "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE" "cancelled exit code"
assert_equal "$RESULT_CANCELLED" "$RESULT_STATE" "cancellation state is distinct"
assert_equal "not_applied" "$RESULT_POLICY_STATUS" "cancelled policy outcome"

result_start
result_success "no batch items"
result_print_json > "$TEST_DIR/empty-items.json"
python3 -m json.tool "$TEST_DIR/empty-items.json" >/dev/null ||
  fail "result JSON without batch items is invalid"
assert_equal "0" "$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(len(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["items"]))' "$TEST_DIR/empty-items.json")" \
  "JSON contains an empty items array for non-batch results"

printf 'All result-model checks passed\n'

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"

FAKE_BIN="$TEST_DIR/bin"
mkdir -p "$FAKE_BIN"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$TEST_DIR"' EXIT

# Setup fake usbguard like in policy_write test
FAKE_RULES_FILE="$TEST_DIR/active_rules.txt"
printf '1: allow id 1d6b:0002\n' > "$FAKE_RULES_FILE"
FAKE_BLOCKED_FILE="$TEST_DIR/blocked_devices.txt"
printf '' > "$FAKE_BLOCKED_FILE"

cat > "$FAKE_BIN/usbguard" <<'MOCK'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
STATE_DIR="${FAKE_STATE_DIR:-/tmp}"
RULES_FILE="$STATE_DIR/active_rules.txt"
BLOCKED_FILE="$STATE_DIR/blocked_devices.txt"
printf '%s\n' "$*" >> "$STATE_DIR/usbguard-commands.log"
case "$1" in
  --version) printf 'USBGuard 1.1.4\n' ;;
  list-devices)
    if [[ "${2:-}" == "--blocked" ]]; then cat "$BLOCKED_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true; exit 0; fi
    printf '1: allow id 1d6b:0002\n'
    ;;
  list-rules) cat "$RULES_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true ;;
  allow-device) exit 0 ;;
  block-device) echo "$2: block" >> "$BLOCKED_FILE"; exit 0 ;;
  append-rule)
    rule=""; for arg in "$@"; do [[ "$arg" != "append-rule" && "$arg" != "--temporary" ]] && rule="$arg"; done
    max_id=$(awk -F: '{gsub(/ /,"",$1); if($1>max) max=$1} END{print max+0}' "$RULES_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
    new_id=$((max_id+1))
    printf '%s: %s\n' "$new_id" "$rule" >> "$RULES_FILE"
    printf '%s\n' "$new_id"
    exit 0
    ;;
  remove-rule)
    rid="${2:-}"; grep -v "^[[:space:]]*$rid:" "$RULES_FILE" > "$RULES_FILE.tmp" || true; mv "$RULES_FILE.tmp" "$RULES_FILE"; exit 0 ;;
  *) exit 64 ;;
esac
MOCK

cat > "$FAKE_BIN/pgrep" <<'MOCK'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
printf '42\n'; exit 0
MOCK

chmod 700 "$FAKE_BIN/usbguard" "$FAKE_BIN/pgrep"
PATH="$FAKE_BIN:$PATH"
export PATH
export FAKE_STATE_DIR="$TEST_DIR"
export USBAM_STATE_DIR="$TEST_DIR/state"
export STATE_DIR="$TEST_DIR/state"
export TTL_FILE="$STATE_DIR/ttl.json"
export CLOCK_STATE_FILE="$STATE_DIR/clock_state.json"
export RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE="$STATE_DIR/restore_journal.json"
mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"

touch "$TEST_DIR/rules.conf"
export USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH

  fail "invalid transition active->pending accepted"

  fail "untrusted initial approval created a TTL record"

# Check status active

  stages_approve_temporary 'allow id abcd:ef03' 3600 \
    'f212db55-5330-4b54-ae96-07582941c7c3' 1
)" || fail "temporary approval with a connected device failed"

  fail "device approval transaction did not reach active TTL state"

  fail "temporary approval did not explicitly approve the connected device"
pass "temporary transaction approves the connected device separately from adding its rule"

# A permanent match must not be duplicated as a temporary TTL rule.
printf 'allow id abcd:ef04\n' >> "$TEST_DIR/rules.conf"
records_before="$(jq '.records | length' "$TTL_FILE")"
if stages_approve_temporary 'allow id abcd:ef04' 3600 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  fail "temporary approval duplicated a persistent rule"
fi
[[ "$STAGES_ERROR_CODE" == "persistent_overlap" ]] ||
  fail "persistent overlap returned an unexpected error code"

  fail "persistent overlap created a TTL record"

  "$TEST_DIR/usbguard-commands.log"; then
  fail "persistent overlap changed active policy"
fi
pass "persistent rules are rejected before creating TTL state or changing policy"

# Test that pending leftover goes to recovery_required in reconcile simulation

# Simulate reconcile - pending should be moved to recovery_required
# We will run cleanup-expired --reconcile logic manually via stages_update_status

#!/usr/bin/env bash

  printf 'FAIL: %s\n' "$1" >&2
  exit 1
}

pass() {
  printf 'PASS: %s\n' "$1"
}

ACTUAL_EPOCH="$(get_current_epoch)" ||
  fail "system Epoch reader did not return a value"

  fail "system Epoch reader returned a non-integer"

MOCK

MOCK
cat > "$FAKE_BIN/ntpq" <<'MOCK'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
[[ "$*" == "-pn" ]] || exit 2
printf 'remote refid st t when poll reach delay offset jitter\n'
case "${FAKE_NTP_PEER_MODE:-valid}" in
  valid) printf '*192.0.2.1 192.0.2.2 2 u 10 64 377 1.0 0.1 0.2\n' ;;
  no-peer) printf '+192.0.2.1 192.0.2.2 2 u 10 64 377 1.0 0.1 0.2\n' ;;
  unreachable) printf '*192.0.2.1 192.0.2.2 2 u 10 64 0 1.0 0.1 0.2\n' ;;
  invalid-stratum) printf '*192.0.2.1 192.0.2.2 16 u 10 64 377 1.0 0.1 0.2\n' ;;
  *) exit 2 ;;
esac
MOCK

clock_sync_evidence ||
  fail "verified active ntpsec peer was rejected"

  "$CLOCK_SYNC_STATUS" == "synchronized" &&
  "$CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE" == "clock_sync_verified" ]] ||

pass "active NTP provider, synchronized system clock, and selected reachable peer are accepted"

  fail "unsynchronized system clock was accepted"

  fail "NTP state without a selected peer was accepted"

  fail "missing selected peer returned an unexpected detail code"
FAKE_NTP_PEER_MODE="unreachable"
if clock_sync_evidence; then
  fail "unreachable selected NTP peer was accepted"
fi
[[ "$CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE" == "clock_sync_peer_unavailable" ]] ||
  fail "unreachable peer returned an unexpected detail code"
FAKE_NTP_PEER_MODE="invalid-stratum"
if clock_sync_evidence; then

[[ "$CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE" == "clock_sync_peer_unavailable" ]] ||
  fail "invalid stratum returned an unexpected detail code"

pass "missing, unreachable, and invalid-stratum NTP peers are rejected"

  fail "inactive NTP provider was accepted"
fi
[[ "$CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE" == "clock_sync_provider_inactive" ]] ||
  fail "inactive provider returned an unexpected detail code"
unset FAKE_NTP_SERVICE

  CLOCK_SYNC_STATUS=""
